[`test/scenario/HeapSettlementBenchmark.t.sol`](test/scenario/HeapSettlementBenchmark.t.sol) only logs gas and asserts no bound.

## Invariant testing
A handler in [`test/invariant/handlers/`](test/invariant/handlers/) drives random bounded actions; every property is checked after each call. `make test-local` runs 1024 runs of depth 32; `fuzz.yml` 4096 runs of depth 64.

### Market solvency — [`test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: supply, withdraw, collateral, borrow, repay, warp, with instant activation.
- [x] total supply assets never fall below total borrow assets — [`invariant_supplyCoversBorrow`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L83)
- [x] the core's loan-token balance covers the idle supply — [`invariant_idleBackedByTokens`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L89)
- [x] the vault forwards every deposit and holds no loan tokens — [`invariant_vaultHoldsNoLoanTokens`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L102)
- [x] the vault owns the whole supply-share book — [`invariant_vaultIsSoleSupplier`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L109)
- [x] the vault's `totalAssets` never exceeds the market's supply — [`invariant_vaultNeverOverReports`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L120)
- [x] the core's collateral balance equals the sum of positions — [`invariant_collateralConservation`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L126)
- [x] no voluntary path leaves debt on active collateral below the floor — [`invariant_noDebtWithSubFloorCollateral`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L141)
- [x] the activation buckets exactly partition each borrower's collateral — [`invariant_activationBucketsPartitionCollateral`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L155)

### Vault conformance — [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol) (18/18), [`test/invariant/VaultConformanceUsdcUsdy.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformanceUsdcUsdy.invariant.t.sol) (6/18)
Actions: market actions, price crashes, liquidations, the ERC-4626 surface, targeted settle, fallback exit, personal targets, donations, pause.
- [x] the vault never claims more than the market's accrued supply owes — [`invariant_noValueCreation`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L124)
- [x] share balances sum to the total supply — [`invariant_holdersSumToSupply`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L144)
- [x] donations stay uncounted and never move the price — [`invariant_donationsAreTheOnlyIdleTokens`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L117), [`invariant_donationNeverMovesPrice`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L240)
- [x] `maxWithdraw` and `maxRedeem` fit free liquidity and are zero while paused — [`invariant_maxAreHonouredBounds`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L157)
- [x] `maxDeposit` and `maxMint` are zero when paused and the cap headroom when capped — [`invariant_maxDepositMintReflectState`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L178)
- [x] `withdraw` rounds the burn up, `redeem` rounds the payout down — [`invariant_standardExitRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L205)
- [x] no exit burns more shares than requested — [`invariant_exitBurnBounded`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L245)
- [x] each exit's value lands within its derived rounding bounds — [`invariant_exitPayoutWithinRoundingBounds`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L268)
- [x] a settle burns exactly `ceil(requested * consumed / claim)` shares — [`invariant_settlementRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L213)
- [x] a targeted settle needs a positive personal slice — [`invariant_targetedSettlementRequiresPersonalEligibility`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L220)
- [x] a targeted settle fits the request's pro-rata claim — [`invariant_targetedSettlementStaysWithinGrossClaim`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L228)
- [x] seized collateral ends at the receiver, not in the vault — [`invariant_settlementCollateralGoesToReceiver`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L233)
- [x] seized collateral is worth at most the cancelled debt — [`invariant_settlementRunsAtPar`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L262), [`invariant_settlePayoutWithinCut`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L274)
- [x] the burned supply is worth at least the cancelled debt — [`invariant_settleBurnCoversDebt`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L280)
- [x] the reported `debtCanceled` equals the removed borrow shares' value — [`invariant_settleDebtReportMatchesDeltas`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L286)
- [x] supply falls by exactly the payout plus the cancelled debt — [`invariant_settleConservesAccounting`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L292)
- [x] idle moves only by the liquid payout and final debt rounding — [`invariant_settleLiquidityMatchesDebtRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L251), [`invariant_fallbackExitIdleMatchesRedeem`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L256)
- [x] settlement-budget accounting never underflows — [`invariant_settlementNeverPanics`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L298)
- [x] under bad debt no settle leg goes through — [`invariant_badDebtGateNeverLeaksASettleLeg`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L305)
- [x] a pause stops every settle path, and only a pause raises its error — [`invariant_pauseStopsEverySettlePath`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L336), [`invariant_pauseErrorOnlyOnAPausedMarket`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L342)

### Exit-book solvency — [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: place, fill, cancel on `Erc4626ExitBook` over a mock vault, with yield, liquidity, pause and time moving.
- [x] the book keeps no assets or vault shares — [`invariant_bookHoldsNoAssets`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L119), [`invariant_bookHoldsNoShares`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L124)
- [x] every fill's payouts add up to the filled amount — [`invariant_payoutConservation`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L129)
- [x] request ids only grow — [`invariant_monotonicIds`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L138)

### Activation inflow — [`test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: credit, voluntary debit and forced seizure over several borrowers in one transaction.
- [x] the inflow accumulator never exceeds its cap — [`invariant_inflowNeverExceedsCap`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol#L34)
- [x] without a lock, dust stays below the threshold and the inactive total above it — [`invariant_noLockActivationBucketsRespectThreshold`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol#L44)

### Guardian signals — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol), [`test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: every registry mutator over two market ids; then the registry with the controller's unlock policy.
- [x] `isBanned` equals latch or strikes at quorum, both ways — [`invariant_banEqualsLatchOrQuorum`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L98)
- [x] opinions, flags and reports equal a model of the spec — [`invariant_registryMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L111)
- [x] the frontier and heartbeats equal the model — [`invariant_frontierMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L124)
- [x] unlock time is start plus base delay plus strike and suspect surcharges — [`invariant_unlockEqualsStartPlusBaseDelayPlusSignalSurcharge`](test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol#L105)

## Simulation testing
One market for 30 days (`test_shortRun_*`, in `make test-local`) or 365 days (`test_longRun_*`, `make test-simulation`), on a fixed and an adaptive IRM; a model predicts every outcome, and books and queue are reconciled every 20 iterations and after the unwind. The tests below are defined in [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol) and run in every suite.

- [x] a WETH/wstETH-shaped market with staking yield and no bad debt — [`test/simulation/WethWstethSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethSolvency.t.sol), [`test/simulation/WethWstethAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol)
- [x] the same market with deep cuts and one crash; keepers write off bad debt — [`test/simulation/WethWstethCrashSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethCrashSolvency.t.sol), [`test/simulation/WethWstethCrashAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethCrashAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol)
- [x] a USDC/USDY-shaped market at 6/18 decimals whose price only rises — [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol), [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdyAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdyAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol)
- [x] one share above the personal allowance reverts — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_personalAllowance_oneShareAboveReverts`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L216)
- [x] a cut below one collateral unit is unavailable — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_seizureFloor_subUnitCutIsUnavailable`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L311)
- [x] a repay that would cross the rate ceiling reverts — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_borrowShareRate_atCeilingReverts`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L265)
- [x] a repay while paused reverts — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_repayWhilePaused_reverts`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L378)
- [x] past failures replay without a write-off or share residue — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_regressionSeed_noWriteOff_repaymentUnwind`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L109), [`test_regressionSeed_shareResidue_forcedExitUnwind`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L124)
- [x] the activation model the runs compare with matches the spec — [`test/unit/ActivationModel.t.sol`](test/unit/ActivationModel.t.sol)

## Fork testing
Flows against a deployed run read from `deployed/<ARTIFACTS_DIR>/`; relative assertions, so they survive governance changes. `make test-fork`.

- [x] the activation rules on the live controller — [`test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol`](test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol): [`testRevert_withdrawCollateral_partOfInactiveLot_InactiveCollateralNotCleared`](test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol#L38), [`test_topUp_beforeMaturity_movesDeadlineToTheNewQuote`](test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol#L64)
- [x] lend, stake, borrow, repay and exit in one run — [`test/fork/HappyPathRoundTrip.t.sol`](test/fork/HappyPathRoundTrip.t.sol): [`test_happyPath_lendBorrowRepayExit`](test/fork/HappyPathRoundTrip.t.sol#L43)
- [x] healthy position, solvent liquidation and socialized loss — [`test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol`](test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol): [`testRevert_liquidate_healthyPosition`](test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol#L30), [`test_liquidateBadDebt_underwater_fullSeizeSocializesResidual`](test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol#L57)
- [x] a leverage loop works only through an instant-activation caller — [`test/fork/WstEthWethLeverageInstantActivation.t.sol`](test/fork/WstEthWethLeverageInstantActivation.t.sol): [`test_leverageLoop_compoundsAllActive_withInstantActivation`](test/fork/WstEthWethLeverageInstantActivation.t.sol#L260), [`testRevert_leverageLoop_revertsWithoutInstantActivation`](test/fork/WstEthWethLeverageInstantActivation.t.sol#L298)
- [x] the WETH/wstETH market on the real wstETH rate, settlement included — [`test/fork/EthStethFork.t.sol`](test/fork/EthStethFork.t.sol): [`test_oracle_readsRealWstEthRate_atFullPrecision`](test/fork/EthStethFork.t.sol#L35); [`test/fork/LidoLendScenario.t.sol`](test/fork/LidoLendScenario.t.sol): [`test_settle_happyPath`](test/fork/LidoLendScenario.t.sol#L251)
- [x] every lender exit path against an independent recompute — [`test/fork/LenderExits.t.sol`](test/fork/LenderExits.t.sol): [`test_vaultRedeem_paysAssetsByErc4626Formula`](test/fork/LenderExits.t.sol#L47), [`test_settle_personalLtv_cutsExactlyTheRecomputedSlice`](test/fork/LenderExits.t.sol#L71); [`test/fork/VaultFallbackExit.t.sol`](test/fork/VaultFallbackExit.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_redeemsAvailableThenSettles`](test/fork/VaultFallbackExit.t.sol#L174)
- [x] both exit books place, fill and cancel — [`test/fork/ExitBooks.t.sol`](test/fork/ExitBooks.t.sol): [`test_morphoBook_fill_paysReceiverAndReward_burnsLiveShares`](test/fork/ExitBooks.t.sol#L113), [`test_morphoBook_cancel_marksCancelled_leavesShares`](test/fork/ExitBooks.t.sol#L152)
- [x] a vault exit through the deployed bundler — [`test/fork/BundledLenderExit.t.sol`](test/fork/BundledLenderExit.t.sol): [`test_bundledFallbackExit_liquidOnly_paysInitiator`](test/fork/BundledLenderExit.t.sol#L43)
- [x] freeze via the dashboard, unfreeze via the facade — [`test/fork/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol`](test/fork/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_freeze_viaSteward_thenUnfreeze_viaFacade_borrowWorksAgain`](test/fork/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L34); [`test/fork/RiskStewardDashboardScenario.t.sol`](test/fork/RiskStewardDashboardScenario.t.sol): [`test_dashboard_freezesRealController_facadeUnfreezes`](test/fork/RiskStewardDashboardScenario.t.sol#L179)
- [x] the single-use CircuitBreaker pause and the Reseal Manager — [`test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_cbPause_registeredPauserPausesForPauseDuration_registrationConsumed`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L59), [`test_resealPauseFor_thenResume`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L97)
- [x] bans, unbans and heartbeats on the live registry — [`test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol`](test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol): [`test_flag_atQuorum_bansAndBlocksBorrow`](test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol#L46), [`test_heartbeat_storesTimestamp_andMovesFrontier`](test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol#L96)
- [x] every lever the executor holds, and the Hoodi Aragon vote — [`test/fork/Governance.t.sol`](test/fork/Governance.t.sol): [`test_supplyCap_depositAtCapPasses_headroomZero`](test/fork/Governance.t.sol#L47); [`test/fork/LendGovernanceVote.t.sol`](test/fork/LendGovernanceVote.t.sol): [`test_addFactoriesVote_executesAndLeavesExecutorOnlyTopology`](test/fork/LendGovernanceVote.t.sol#L98)
- [x] one market's actions leave another untouched — [`test/fork/MultiMarketIsolation.t.sol`](test/fork/MultiMarketIsolation.t.sol): [`test_flowOnSecondMarket_leavesFirstUntouched`](test/fork/MultiMarketIsolation.t.sol#L43), [`test_resealPauseOnOne_leavesOtherLive`](test/fork/MultiMarketIsolation.t.sol#L61)
- [x] an outage of the real price source halts only priced actions — [`test/fork/OracleOutage.t.sol`](test/fork/OracleOutage.t.sol): [`testRevert_oracleDown_borrow`](test/fork/OracleOutage.t.sol#L42), [`test_oracleDown_vaultDepositAndWithdrawWithinLiquidity`](test/fork/OracleOutage.t.sol#L94)
- [x] the USDY blocklist blocks liquidation and exit to a listed address — [`test/fork/UsdyTreasuriesMarket.t.sol`](test/fork/UsdyTreasuriesMarket.t.sol): [`test_liquidate_blocklistedCannot_allowlistedCan`](test/fork/UsdyTreasuriesMarket.t.sol#L175), [`test_fallbackExit_blocklistedReceiverCannot_allowlistedCan`](test/fork/UsdyTreasuriesMarket.t.sol#L225)
- [x] a Vault V2 allocates into a market and force-deallocates — [`test/fork/MetaMorphoV2Integration.t.sol`](test/fork/MetaMorphoV2Integration.t.sol): [`test_metaMorphoV2_allocatesIntoForkAndForceDeallocates`](test/fork/MetaMorphoV2Integration.t.sol#L120)

## Acceptance testing
One market of a deploy run, on a fork. Expected values come from `deployed/<run>/`, the motion's choices from `ACCEPTANCE_PAUSER_COMMITTEE` and `ACCEPTANCE_ENABLE_SETTLEMENT`. `make acceptance-run RUN=<run> MARKET=<market>`.

1. Before the activation motion — [`test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol); skips once the market is activated
   - [x] bytecode, wiring, config and exact role holders match the artifacts; the market is unbound and its accounting zero — [`test_deployedInertAndReadyForMotion`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L22)
   - [x] the exit-book pauser can pause both books — [`test_exitBookPauserPausesBothBooks`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L37)
   - [x] rejects a paused or frozen controller — [`testRevert_pausedControllerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L41), [`testRevert_frozenControllerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L67)
   - [x] rejects a wrong core owner or wrong bytecode — [`testRevert_wrongCoreOwnerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L48), [`testRevert_wrongFacadeBytecodeIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L55), [`testRevert_wrongCircuitBreakerBytecodeIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L61)
   - [x] rejects an extra instant-activation caller or market steward — [`testRevert_unexpectedInstantActivationCallerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L76), [`testRevert_unexpectedMarketStewardIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L86)
   - [x] rejects nonzero vault supply, a nonempty settlement queue or a wrong vault symbol — [`testRevert_nonZeroVaultSupplyIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L95), [`testRevert_nonZeroSettlementQueueIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L101), [`testRevert_wrongVaultSymbolIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L107)
2. After the motion — [`test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol); skips before activation
   - [x] the market is created, bound and routed, with exact role holders — [`test_fullyConnectedConfiguredAndLive`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol#L18)
   - [x] the registered CircuitBreaker pauser pauses the controller and the registration clears — [`test_circuitBreakerPausesControllerAndClearsRegistration`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol#L29)
   - [x] the exit-book pauser pauses both books — [`test_exitBookPauserPausesBothBooks`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol#L33)
3. A second activation — [`test/acceptance/MultiMarketActivationSimulation.t.sol`](test/acceptance/MultiMarketActivationSimulation.t.sol) (`make acceptance-run-multi`)
   - [x] activating market B leaves market A's routing, controller, pauser and allowlists intact — [`test_twoMarketsActivateWithoutCrossMarketStateCollision`](test/acceptance/MultiMarketActivationSimulation.t.sol#L8)
4. Stages not covered
   - [ ] a deployment with the backbone but no market (the suite needs `MARKET`)
   - [ ] a live market whose exit books do not allow it yet
   - [ ] a market after a real CircuitBreaker pause consumed the registration
   - [ ] role changes after launch ([`script/DEPLOYMENT.md`](script/DEPLOYMENT.md))

## state-mate
On-chain state against reviewed YAML, at any time. Configs come from [`script/config/state-mate/generate.py`](script/config/state-mate/generate.py) (`make state-mate-config RUN=<run> RPC_URL=<node>`); state-mate checks one with `yarn start <config>`, as `.github/workflows/pending-action.yml` does.

- [x] Hoodi, right after deploy: roles, parameters, proxies; markets inert — [`script/config/state-mate/hoodi-post-deploy.yaml`](script/config/state-mate/hoodi-post-deploy.yaml)
- [x] Hoodi, after the activation replay on a fork — [`script/config/state-mate/hoodi-post-activation-fork.yaml`](script/config/state-mate/hoodi-post-activation-fork.yaml)
- [x] Hoodi, live after the pending motions — [`script/config/state-mate/hoodi-testnet.yaml`](script/config/state-mate/hoodi-testnet.yaml)
- [ ] mainnet configs (the generator reads `HOODI_RPC_URL`)
- [ ] the market factory, `IrmConfigFactory` and IRM configs, the bundler and adapters
- [ ] committee Safes' threshold and owners

## Unreachable branches
Coverage misses two `src/` branches, on forge 1.7.1 and 1.8.3 alike.

- [-] `UnknownHookEvent` ([`src/controllers/LidoLendMarketController.sol:183`](src/controllers/LidoLendMarketController.sol#L183)): the ABI decoder rejects an out-of-range `HookEvent` first — [`test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol): [`test_on_outOfRangeHookEvent_isRejectedAtDecode`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L393)
- [-] `price == 0` in the dust check ([`src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol:278`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L278)): its only caller returns earlier on a zero collateral value (line 163)
- [x] `quorum == 0` in the frontier sync ([`src/GuardianSignals.sol:395`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L395)) holds on an uninitialized registry; `n < quorum` cannot hold — [`test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol): [`test_frontier_uninitializedRegistry_syncIsNoOp`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L676)
